# VideoFactory 界面文案静态清单（机械抽取）

- apps/studio/src/client/onboarding/creator-tour-steps.ts:24 [literal] "欢迎来到今日创作"

- apps/studio/src/client/onboarding/creator-tour-steps.ts:43 [literal] "采用候选，才会进入制作区"
